========== Q1(a) IntelLedger named funcs + same-file callees ==========

CALL_GRAPH_CLOSURE=_actor_present,_visible_samples,_summarize_board,_ids_ok,_sample_visible,_clear_last,_record,_is_broker_qualified,_direction_of,_mul_checked,_add_checked,_expect_int,_dict_int

### IntelLedger._actor_present
FILE=autoload/intel_ledger.gd LINES=550-566 TOTAL_LINES=17
```gdscript
func _actor_present(actor_id: StringName, port_id: StringName) -> bool:
	## FRM-SYS15-06: docked ship at P, or any-state building at P.
	if ShipRegistry.is_loaded():
		for sid_variant in ShipRegistry.ship_ids(actor_id):
			var info: Dictionary = ShipRegistry.ship_info(sid_variant)
			if info.is_empty():
				continue
			if StringName(info.get("state", &"")) != Ship.STATE_DOCKED:
				continue
			if String(info.get("port_id", "")) == String(port_id):
				return true
	if BuildingEconomy.is_loaded():
		for b_variant in BuildingEconomy.buildings_for_port(String(port_id)):
			var bld: Building = b_variant as Building
			if bld != null and bld.owner == actor_id:
				return true
	return false
```

### IntelLedger._visible_samples
FILE=autoload/intel_ledger.gd LINES=593-630 TOTAL_LINES=38
```gdscript
func _visible_samples(
	actor_id: StringName, port_id: StringName, origin_port: StringName, good_id: StringName
) -> Array:
	var window_d: int = Params.get_int(StringName(PRM_WINDOW_DAYS))
	var today: int = Clock.day
	var board: Dictionary = _rec.get(String(port_id), {})
	var out: Array = []
	var pair_keys: Array = board.keys()
	pair_keys.sort()
	for pair_variant in pair_keys:
		var entry: Dictionary = board[pair_variant]
		var snap: Dictionary = entry.get(KEY_SNAPSHOT, {})
		if String(snap.get(KEY_ORIGIN_PORT, "")) != String(origin_port):
			continue
		var tick_arrived: int = _dict_int(entry, KEY_TICK_ARRIVED, 0)
		if not _sample_visible(actor_id, port_id, tick_arrived):
			continue
		var day_taken: int = _dict_int(snap, KEY_DAY_TAKEN, 0)
		var age: int = today - day_taken
		if age < 0 or age >= window_d:
			continue
		var goods: Dictionary = snap.get(KEY_GOODS, {})
		if not goods.has(String(good_id)):
			continue
		var g: Dictionary = goods[String(good_id)]
		out.append({
			KEY_SHIP_ID: snap.get(KEY_SHIP_ID, &""),
			KEY_OWNER_ID: snap.get(KEY_OWNER_ID, &""),
			KEY_ORIGIN_PORT: origin_port,
			KEY_TICK_TAKEN: _dict_int(snap, KEY_TICK_TAKEN, 0),
			KEY_DAY_TAKEN: day_taken,
			KEY_MID_LI: _dict_int(g, KEY_MID_LI, 0),
			KEY_BAND: _dict_int(g, KEY_BAND, 0),
			KEY_WEEKS_X: g.get(KEY_WEEKS_X, null),
			KEY_TICK_ARRIVED: tick_arrived,
			KEY_AGE_DAYS: age,
		})
	return out
```

### IntelLedger._summarize_board
FILE=autoload/intel_ledger.gd LINES=633-761 TOTAL_LINES=129
```gdscript
func _summarize_board(samples: Array, good_id: StringName) -> Dictionary:
	var n: int = samples.size()
	if n == 0:
		return {
			KEY_N: 0,
			KEY_CONSENSUS_MID: null,
			KEY_BAND: null,
			KEY_CONSENSUS_WEEKS: null,
			KEY_MEAN_AGE: null,
			KEY_NEWEST_AGE: null,
			KEY_DIV_PPM: null,
			KEY_DIV_BAND: null,
			KEY_DIRECTION: null,
		}
	var decay_d: int = Params.get_int(StringName(PRM_DECAY_DAYS))
	var ppm: int = Params.get_int(StringName(PRM_PPM_SCALE))
	var sum_w: int = 0
	var sum_wm: int = 0
	var sum_wa: int = 0
	var sum_ww: int = 0
	var sum_w_weeks: int = 0
	var min_mid: int = 0
	var max_mid: int = 0
	var newest_age: int = 0
	var first := true
	for s_variant in samples:
		var s: Dictionary = s_variant
		var age: int = _dict_int(s, KEY_AGE_DAYS, 0)
		var w: int = decay_d - age
		var mid: int = _dict_int(s, KEY_MID_LI, 0)
		var prod_wm: Variant = _mul_checked(w, mid)
		var prod_wa: Variant = _mul_checked(w, age)
		if prod_wm == null or prod_wa == null:
			_record(ERR_SYS15_07, KEY_CONSENSUS_MID, mid, "weighted sum overflow")
			return {}
		var prod_wm_i: int = _expect_int(prod_wm, KEY_CONSENSUS_MID)
		var prod_wa_i: int = _expect_int(prod_wa, KEY_MEAN_AGE)
		if not _add_checked(sum_w, w) or not _add_checked(sum_wm, prod_wm_i):
			_record(ERR_SYS15_07, KEY_CONSENSUS_MID, mid, "weighted sum overflow")
			return {}
		if not _add_checked(sum_wa, prod_wa_i):
			_record(ERR_SYS15_07, KEY_MEAN_AGE, age, "weighted age overflow")
			return {}
		sum_w = sum_w + w
		sum_wm = sum_wm + prod_wm_i
		sum_wa = sum_wa + prod_wa_i
		var weeks_x: Variant = s.get(KEY_WEEKS_X, null)
		if weeks_x != null:
			var weeks_i: int = _expect_int(weeks_x, KEY_CONSENSUS_WEEKS)
			var prod_ww: Variant = _mul_checked(w, weeks_i)
			if prod_ww == null:
				_record(ERR_SYS15_07, KEY_CONSENSUS_WEEKS, weeks_x, "weeks weighted overflow")
				return {}
			var prod_ww_i: int = _expect_int(prod_ww, KEY_CONSENSUS_WEEKS)
			if not _add_checked(sum_ww, prod_ww_i):
				_record(ERR_SYS15_07, KEY_CONSENSUS_WEEKS, weeks_x, "weeks weighted overflow")
				return {}
			if not _add_checked(sum_w_weeks, w):
				_record(ERR_SYS15_07, KEY_CONSENSUS_WEEKS, weeks_x, "weeks weight overflow")
				return {}
			sum_ww = sum_ww + prod_ww_i
			sum_w_weeks = sum_w_weeks + w
		if first:
			min_mid = mid
			max_mid = mid
			newest_age = age
			first = false
		else:
			if mid < min_mid:
				min_mid = mid
			if mid > max_mid:
				max_mid = mid
			if age < newest_age:
				newest_age = age
	if sum_w <= 0:
		return {
			KEY_N: 0,
			KEY_CONSENSUS_MID: null,
			KEY_BAND: null,
			KEY_CONSENSUS_WEEKS: null,
			KEY_MEAN_AGE: null,
			KEY_NEWEST_AGE: null,
			KEY_DIV_PPM: null,
			KEY_DIV_BAND: null,
			KEY_DIRECTION: null,
		}
	## A27／FRM-SYS15-03／C-09: integer ÷ integer, no float.
	var consensus: int = sum_wm / sum_w
	var mean_age: int = sum_wa / sum_w
	var consensus_weeks: Variant = null
	if sum_w_weeks > 0:
		consensus_weeks = sum_ww / sum_w_weeks
	var good: GoodDef = GoodCatalog.get_good(String(good_id))
	var base_li: int = good.base_price_li
	var band: int = MarketPricer.indicator_band(
		float(consensus) / float(base_li) if base_li > 0 else 0.0
	)
	var div_ppm: int = 0
	var div_band: int = DivergenceBand.LOW
	if n >= MIN_SAMPLES_FOR_TREND:
		var span: int = max_mid - min_mid
		var prod_div: Variant = _mul_checked(span, ppm)
		if prod_div == null:
			_record(ERR_SYS15_07, KEY_DIV_PPM, span, "divergence overflow")
			return {}
		var prod_div_i: int = _expect_int(prod_div, KEY_DIV_PPM)
		## A27／FRM-SYS15-04／C-09: integer ÷ consensus_mid_li.
		if consensus > 0:
			div_ppm = prod_div_i / consensus
		var lo: int = Params.get_int(StringName(PRM_DIV_LO))
		var hi: int = Params.get_int(StringName(PRM_DIV_HI))
		if div_ppm <= lo:
			div_band = DivergenceBand.LOW
		elif div_ppm <= hi:
			div_band = DivergenceBand.MID
		else:
			div_band = DivergenceBand.HIGH
	var direction: StringName = _direction_of(samples, ppm)
	return {
		KEY_N: n,
		KEY_CONSENSUS_MID: consensus,
		KEY_BAND: band,
		KEY_CONSENSUS_WEEKS: consensus_weeks,
		KEY_MEAN_AGE: mean_age,
		KEY_NEWEST_AGE: newest_age,
		KEY_DIV_PPM: div_ppm,
		KEY_DIV_BAND: div_band,
		KEY_DIRECTION: direction,
	}
```

### IntelLedger._ids_ok
FILE=autoload/intel_ledger.gd LINES=531-547 TOTAL_LINES=17
```gdscript
func _ids_ok(port_id: StringName, origin_port: StringName, good_id: StringName) -> bool:
	if not PortRegistry.is_loaded() or not GoodCatalog.is_loaded():
		return false
	var port_ok := false
	var origin_ok := false
	for p in PortRegistry.all_ids():
		var ps: String = String(p)
		if ps == String(port_id):
			port_ok = true
		if ps == String(origin_port):
			origin_ok = true
	if not port_ok or not origin_ok:
		return false
	for g in GoodCatalog.all_ids():
		if String(g) == String(good_id):
			return true
	return false
```

### IntelLedger._sample_visible
FILE=autoload/intel_ledger.gd LINES=583-590 TOTAL_LINES=8
```gdscript
func _sample_visible(actor_id: StringName, port_id: StringName, tick_arrived: int) -> bool:
	## FRM-SYS16-04: visible if tick ≥ arrived+delay, or broker-qualified.
	var delay: int = Params.get_int(StringName(PRM_PUBLIC_DELAY))
	if Clock.tick >= tick_arrived + delay:
		return true
	if delay == 0:
		return true
	return _is_broker_qualified(actor_id, port_id)
```

```gdscript
func _record(code: StringName, field: String, value: Variant, message: String) -> void:
	_last_error = ErrorInfoUtil.make_validation(code, SYS_ID, SCRIPT_PATH, field, value, message)
	push_error("IntelLedger: %s" % str(_last_error))
```

```gdscript
func _is_broker_qualified(actor_id: StringName, port_id: StringName) -> bool:
	## FRM-SYS16-04／02／D-60f: single rule via injected BrokerProvider（N-04）.
	if _broker == null:
		return false
	return _broker.is_broker_qualified(actor_id, port_id)
```

### IntelLedger._direction_of
FILE=autoload/intel_ledger.gd LINES=764-821 TOTAL_LINES=58
```gdscript
func _direction_of(samples: Array, ppm: int) -> StringName:
	## FRM-SYS15-05.
	if samples.size() < MIN_SAMPLES_FOR_TREND:
		return DIR_NONE
	var ordered: Array = samples.duplicate()
	ordered.sort_custom(func(a: Dictionary, b: Dictionary) -> bool:
		var da: int = _dict_int(a, KEY_DAY_TAKEN, 0)
		var db: int = _dict_int(b, KEY_DAY_TAKEN, 0)
		if da != db:
			return da > db
		var ta: int = _dict_int(a, KEY_TICK_TAKEN, 0)
		var tb: int = _dict_int(b, KEY_TICK_TAKEN, 0)
		if ta != tb:
			return ta > tb
		return String(a[KEY_SHIP_ID]) < String(b[KEY_SHIP_ID])
	)
	var newest: Dictionary = ordered[0]
	var rest: Array = ordered.slice(1)
	var decay_d: int = Params.get_int(StringName(PRM_DECAY_DAYS))
	var sum_w: int = 0
	var sum_wm: int = 0
	for s_variant in rest:
		var s: Dictionary = s_variant
		var age: int = _dict_int(s, KEY_AGE_DAYS, 0)
		var w: int = decay_d - age
		var mid: int = _dict_int(s, KEY_MID_LI, 0)
		var prod: Variant = _mul_checked(w, mid)
		if prod == null:
			_record(ERR_SYS15_07, KEY_DIRECTION, mid, "direction rest overflow")
			return DIR_NONE
		var prod_i: int = _expect_int(prod, KEY_DIRECTION)
		if not _add_checked(sum_w, w) or not _add_checked(sum_wm, prod_i):
			_record(ERR_SYS15_07, KEY_DIRECTION, mid, "direction rest overflow")
			return DIR_NONE
		sum_w = sum_w + w
		sum_wm = sum_wm + prod_i
	if sum_w <= 0:
		return DIR_NONE
	## A27／FRM-SYS15-05／C-09: rest consensus via integer ÷.
	var rest_mid: int = sum_wm / sum_w
	var mid_n: int = _dict_int(newest, KEY_MID_LI, 0)
	var d: int = mid_n - rest_mid
	var abs_d: int = d if d >= 0 else 0 - d
	var deadband: int = Params.get_int(StringName(PRM_DEADBAND))
	var left: Variant = _mul_checked(abs_d, ppm)
	var right: Variant = _mul_checked(deadband, rest_mid)
	if left == null or right == null:
		_record(ERR_SYS15_07, KEY_DIRECTION, d, "direction deadband overflow")
		return DIR_NONE
	var left_i: int = _expect_int(left, KEY_DIRECTION)
	var right_i: int = _expect_int(right, KEY_DIRECTION)
	if left_i <= right_i:
		return DIR_FLAT
	if d > 0:
		return DIR_UP
	if d < 0:
		return DIR_DOWN
	return DIR_FLAT
```

### IntelLedger._mul_checked
FILE=autoload/intel_ledger.gd LINES=824-832 TOTAL_LINES=9
```gdscript
func _mul_checked(a: int, b: int) -> Variant:
	## C-10: detect int64 wrap without magic INT64_MAX literal.
	## Returns int（TYPE_INT）or null — never float（C-09）.
	if a == 0 or b == 0:
		return 0
	var p: int = a * b
	if p / a != b:
		return null
	return p
```

### IntelLedger._add_checked
FILE=autoload/intel_ledger.gd LINES=835-840 TOTAL_LINES=6
```gdscript
func _add_checked(a: int, b: int) -> bool:
	if b == 0:
		return true
	if b > 0:
		return a <= a + b
	return a >= a + b
```

### IntelLedger._expect_int
FILE=autoload/intel_ledger.gd LINES=843-848 TOTAL_LINES=6
```gdscript
func _expect_int(v: Variant, field: String) -> int:
	## C-09: reject float→int coercion；only accept already-integer Variants.
	if typeof(v) != TYPE_INT:
		_record(ERR_SYS15_07, field, v, "non-int where int required (C-09)")
		return 0
	return v
```

### IntelLedger._dict_int
FILE=autoload/intel_ledger.gd LINES=851-859 TOTAL_LINES=9
```gdscript
func _dict_int(d: Dictionary, key: String, default_v: int) -> int:
	## C-09: Dictionary values written as int／null only；prove TYPE_INT before use.
	if not d.has(key):
		return default_v
	var v: Variant = d[key]
	if typeof(v) != TYPE_INT:
		_record(ERR_SYS15_07, key, v, "non-int dict value (C-09)")
		return default_v
	return v
```

### DemandModel.weekly_potential_demand
FILE=core/demand_model.gd LINES=149-161 TOTAL_LINES=13
```gdscript
func weekly_potential_demand(port_id: StringName, good_id: StringName) -> int:
	## D-96f／FRM-SYS23-04／FRM-SYS04-01（改）：週潛在需求（擔／週，FLOOR）。
	## FRM-SYS08-25／D-109(h)：floori(口徑) ＋ ceili(缺料 ÷ QUEUE_MAT_DEMAND_WEEKS).
	## Provider path allows EX-04-09 fixtures before DemandModel load (R9-65 A2).
	if _potential_demand_provider != null:
		assert(
			_potential_demand_provider.has_method("weekly_potential_demand"),
			"potential demand provider missing weekly_potential_demand"
		)
		return _potential_demand_provider.weekly_potential_demand(port_id, good_id)
	assert(_loaded, "DemandModel.weekly_potential_demand before load")
	var base: int = weekly_demand(port_id, good_id)
	return base + _queue_mat_demand_addon(port_id, good_id)
```

========== Q1(c) MarketPricer.mid_price + callees + quote ==========

MID_PRICE_CLOSURE=mid_price,_clear_last_error,_fail_from_ctx,_floor_mult,_m_of_w,_resolve_context,_stock_weeks_raw,_record_error,_err

### MarketPricer.mid_price
FILE=autoload/market_pricer.gd LINES=158-172 TOTAL_LINES=15
```gdscript
func mid_price(port_id: StringName, good_id: StringName) -> float:
	# FRM-SYS04-03: mid = anchor × m(w); w via single weeks formula (D-34d／N-04).
	assert(_loaded, "MarketPricer.mid_price before load_and_validate")
	_clear_last_error()
	var ctx: Dictionary = _resolve_context(port_id, good_id)
	if ctx.has(KEY_ERROR):
		_fail_from_ctx(ctx)
		return 0.0
	var anchor: int = int(ctx[FIELD_ANCHOR])
	# stock／weekly_offtake written as int／float in _context（C-09: no float→int via int()）.
	var stock_mid: int = ctx["stock"]
	var weekly_mid: float = ctx["weekly_offtake"]
	var w: Variant = _stock_weeks_raw(stock_mid, weekly_mid)
	var m: float = _floor_mult() if w == null else _m_of_w(float(w))
	return float(anchor) * m
```

```gdscript
func _fail_from_ctx(ctx: Dictionary) -> void:
	var code: StringName = StringName(ctx[KEY_ERROR])
	var field: String = String(ctx.get("field", KEY_ERROR))
	var value: Variant = ctx.get("value", null)
	var message: String = String(ctx.get("message", str(code)))
	_record_error(code, field, value, message)
```

### MarketPricer._floor_mult
FILE=autoload/market_pricer.gd LINES=402-404 TOTAL_LINES=3
```gdscript
func _floor_mult() -> float:
	var last_i: int = _curve.point_count() - 1
	return _curve.mults[last_i]
```

### MarketPricer._m_of_w
FILE=autoload/market_pricer.gd LINES=384-399 TOTAL_LINES=16
```gdscript
func _m_of_w(w: float) -> float:
	# FRM-SYS04-02: linear interpolation on breakpoints; w ≥ W_n → M_n
	var n: int = _curve.point_count()
	var last_i: int = n - 1
	if w >= _curve.weeks[last_i]:
		return _curve.mults[last_i]
	var i: int = 0
	while i < last_i:
		var w_i: float = _curve.weeks[i]
		var w_j: float = _curve.weeks[i + 1]
		if w_i <= w and w < w_j:
			var m_i: float = _curve.mults[i]
			var m_j: float = _curve.mults[i + 1]
			return m_i + (m_j - m_i) * (w - w_i) / (w_j - w_i)
		i = i + 1
	return _curve.mults[0]
```

### MarketPricer._resolve_context
FILE=autoload/market_pricer.gd LINES=272-370 TOTAL_LINES=99
```gdscript
func _resolve_context(port_id: StringName, good_id: StringName) -> Dictionary:
	var port_s: String = String(port_id)
	var good_s: String = String(good_id)

	# A21：先驗港口存在，再碰 GoodsLedger.make_market（查詢會登記容器）.
	if not PortRegistry.is_loaded():
		return {
			KEY_ERROR: CODE_UNKNOWN_PORT,
			"field": FIELD_PORT_ID,
			"value": port_s,
			"message": "PortRegistry not loaded",
		}
	var port_def: PortDef = PortRegistry.get_port(port_s)
	if port_def == null:
		return {
			KEY_ERROR: CODE_UNKNOWN_PORT,
			"field": FIELD_PORT_ID,
			"value": port_s,
			"message": "unknown port_id",
		}

	if not GoodCatalog.is_loaded():
		return {KEY_ERROR: CODE_UNKNOWN_GOOD, "field": "good_id", "value": good_s, "message": "GoodCatalog not loaded"}
	var good_found := false
	for id_variant in GoodCatalog.all_ids():
		if String(id_variant) == good_s:
			good_found = true
			break
	if not good_found:
		return {KEY_ERROR: CODE_UNKNOWN_GOOD, "field": "good_id", "value": good_s, "message": "unknown good_id"}

	var good: GoodDef = GoodCatalog.get_good(good_s)
	# FRM-SYS04-11: anchor always GoodDef.base_price_li (all ports／goods).
	var anchor: int = good.base_price_li

	if not GoodsLedger.is_loaded():
		return {
			KEY_ERROR: CODE_GOODS_LEDGER,
			"field": FIELD_GOODS_LEDGER,
			"value": null,
			"message": "GoodsLedger not loaded",
		}

	# FRM-SYS04-01（改）: previous-day offtake snapshot; empty → window_qty 0 (floor).
	# A22／C-06：非空快照缺 window_days 或 <1 不得靜默補 1.
	var snap: Dictionary = GoodsLedger.offtake_snapshot(port_id, good_id)
	var window_qty: int = 0
	var window_days: int = 1
	if not snap.is_empty():
		window_qty = int(snap.get(FIELD_WINDOW_QTY, 0))
		if not snap.has(FIELD_WINDOW_DAYS):
			return {
				KEY_ERROR: CODE_BAD_OFFTAKE,
				"field": FIELD_WINDOW_DAYS,
				"value": null,
				"message": "offtake snapshot missing window_days",
			}
		var wd_v: Variant = snap[FIELD_WINDOW_DAYS]
		if typeof(wd_v) != TYPE_INT:
			return {
				KEY_ERROR: CODE_BAD_OFFTAKE,
				"field": FIELD_WINDOW_DAYS,
				"value": wd_v,
				"message": "offtake window_days must be int",
			}
		window_days = int(wd_v)
		if window_days < 1:
			return {
				KEY_ERROR: CODE_BAD_OFFTAKE,
				"field": FIELD_WINDOW_DAYS,
				"value": window_days,
				"message": "offtake window_days must be >= 1",
			}

	var weekly_offtake: float = 0.0
	if window_qty > 0:
		# Cast to float only when computing weekly_offtake／w (15-03).
		var days_pw: int = Params.get_int(PRM_DAYS_PER_WEEK)
		weekly_offtake = float(window_qty) * float(days_pw) / float(window_days)

	## D-96(a)／FRM-SYS04-01（改）：weekly_basis = MAX(週去化, 週潛在需求).
	var potential_dan: int = 0
	if DemandModel.has_potential_demand_provider() or DemandModel.is_loaded():
		potential_dan = DemandModel.weekly_potential_demand(port_id, good_id)
	var weekly_basis: float = weekly_offtake
	if float(potential_dan) > weekly_basis:
		weekly_basis = float(potential_dan)

	var market: Dictionary = GoodsLedger.make_market(port_s)
	var stock: int = GoodsLedger.get_qty(market, good_id)
	return {
		FIELD_ANCHOR: anchor,
		"stock": stock,
		"weekly_offtake": weekly_basis, ## key kept for callers; value is weekly_basis (D-96a)
		"weekly_basis": weekly_basis,
		"weekly_offtake_raw": weekly_offtake,
		"weekly_potential": potential_dan,
		FIELD_OFFTAKE: snap,
	}
```

### MarketPricer._stock_weeks_raw
FILE=autoload/market_pricer.gd LINES=373-381 TOTAL_LINES=9
```gdscript
func _stock_weeks_raw(stock: int, weekly_offtake: float) -> Variant:
	## D-96(a)／FRM-SYS04-01（改）／09（改） single site (TST-SYS15-19／N-04):
	## MARKET stock＝0 → w＝0（天花板）；stock>0 且 weekly_basis＝0 → null（地板）；
	## else w＝stock÷weekly_basis.
	if stock == 0:
		return 0.0
	if weekly_offtake <= 0.0:
		return null
	return float(stock) / weekly_offtake
```

### MarketPricer.quote
FILE=autoload/market_pricer.gd LINES=175-232 TOTAL_LINES=58
```gdscript
func quote(port_id: StringName, good_id: StringName, side: int, qty: int) -> Dictionary:
	# FRM-SYS04-04／05 + D-01: batch area; quantize_amount then CEIL buy／FLOOR sell.
	assert(_loaded, "MarketPricer.quote before load_and_validate")
	_clear_last_error()
	var out: Dictionary = {
		KEY_TOTAL_LI: 0,
		KEY_AVG_LI: 0.0,
		KEY_MID_AFTER: 0.0,
		KEY_BAND_AFTER: BAND_EQUILIBRIUM,
	}

	if side != SIDE_BUY and side != SIDE_SELL:
		out[KEY_ERROR] = ERR_SYS04_06
		_record_error(ERR_SYS04_06, FIELD_SIDE, side, "invalid side")
		return out

	# ERR-SYS04-06 — q ≤ 0 or buy qty > stock
	if qty <= 0:
		out[KEY_ERROR] = ERR_SYS04_06
		_record_error(ERR_SYS04_06, FIELD_QTY, qty, "qty<=0")
		return out

	var ctx: Dictionary = _resolve_context(port_id, good_id)
	if ctx.has(KEY_ERROR):
		out[KEY_ERROR] = ctx[KEY_ERROR]
		_fail_from_ctx(ctx)
		return out

	var anchor: int = int(ctx[FIELD_ANCHOR])
	var stock: int = int(ctx["stock"])
	var weekly_offtake: float = float(ctx["weekly_offtake"])

	if side == SIDE_BUY and qty > stock:
		out[KEY_ERROR] = ERR_SYS04_06
		_record_error(ERR_SYS04_06, FIELD_QTY, qty, "buy qty>%s stock" % stock)
		return out

	var stock_after: int = stock - qty if side == SIDE_BUY else stock + qty
	var area: float = batch_area(anchor, weekly_offtake, stock, stock_after)
	var half: float = Params.get_float(PRM_HALF_SPREAD)
	var total: int = 0
	if side == SIDE_BUY:
		# FRM-SYS04-05 + D-01: CEIL(quantize(Area × (1 + HALF_SPREAD)))
		total = ceili(AmountQuantize.quantize_amount(area * (EQ_MULT + half)))
	else:
		# FRM-SYS04-05 + D-01: FLOOR(quantize(Area × (1 − HALF_SPREAD)))
		total = floori(AmountQuantize.quantize_amount(area * (EQ_MULT - half)))

	var w_after: Variant = _stock_weeks_raw(stock_after, weekly_offtake)
	var mid_after: float = (
		float(anchor) * _floor_mult() if w_after == null else float(anchor) * _m_of_w(float(w_after))
	)
	var ratio: float = mid_after / float(anchor) if anchor > 0 else 0.0
	out[KEY_TOTAL_LI] = total
	out[KEY_AVG_LI] = float(total) / float(qty)
	out[KEY_MID_AFTER] = mid_after
	out[KEY_BAND_AFTER] = indicator_band(ratio)
	return out
```

========== Q1(d) PopulationModel opening placement ==========

### PopulationModel.apply_opening_init
FILE=autoload/population_model.gd LINES=340-384 TOTAL_LINES=45
```gdscript
func apply_opening_init(opening_pop_dir: String = OPENING_POP_DIR) -> Array:
	## FRM-SYS07-14／D-19e／D-87: validate all opening rows first; any error → no writes.
	## A17: sorted load; unknown port／bad resource collected (C-06), not silent.
	assert(_loaded, "PopulationModel.apply_opening_init before load")
	var errors: Array = []
	var rows: Array = _load_opening_population(opening_pop_dir, errors)
	var seen: Dictionary = {} ## port_id → true
	var pending: Array = [] ## OpeningPopulation rows ready to apply
	if rows.is_empty() and errors.is_empty():
		errors.append(
			_err(ERR_SYS07_09, "opening_population", opening_pop_dir, "opening population dir empty or missing")
		)
	for row_v in rows:
		var row: OpeningPopulation = row_v
		var pid: String = String(row.port_id)
		if seen.has(pid):
			errors.append(_err(ERR_SYS07_09, "port_id", pid, "duplicate opening population port"))
			continue
		seen[pid] = true
		if row.poor < 0 or row.citizen < 0 or row.rich < 0:
			errors.append(_err(ERR_SYS07_01, "opening_pop", pid, "negative opening population"))
			continue
		if PortRegistry.is_loaded() and not _port_registry_has(pid):
			errors.append(_err(ERR_SYS07_09, "port_id", pid, "unknown opening population port"))
			continue
		pending.append(row)
	if PortRegistry.is_loaded():
		for pid_v in PortRegistry.all_ids():
			var want: String = String(pid_v)
			if not seen.has(want):
				errors.append(_err(ERR_SYS07_09, "port_id", want, "missing opening population for port"))
	if not errors.is_empty():
		_emit(errors)
		return errors
	## D-87: writes only after full validation passed.
	for row2_v in pending:
		var row2: OpeningPopulation = row2_v
		var pid2: String = String(row2.port_id)
		set_port_state(pid2, row2.poor, row2.citizen, row2.rich, 0)
		var p: PortPop = _ensure_port(pid2)
		p.streak_up1 = 0
		p.streak_up2 = 0
		p.acc = {}
		p.pending_release_workers = 0
	return errors
```

### PopulationModel._daily_port (call site of pop.opening_place / _opening_place_full)
FILE=autoload/population_model.gd LINES=547-612 TOTAL_LINES=66
```gdscript
func _daily_port(port_id: String, day: int, season: int, errors: Array) -> void:
	var p: PortPop = _ensure_port(port_id)
	_draw_seq_by_purpose = {}
	# ① S0
	var s0_poor: int = p.poor
	var s0_cit: int = p.citizen
	var s0_rich: int = p.rich
	var s0_beg: int = p.beggar
	var s0_sat_p: int = p.sat_poor
	var s0_sat_c: int = p.sat_citizen
	var s0_sat_r: int = p.sat_rich
	# FRM-SYS07-14／D-63e: one full local placement before first movers (S0 counts).
	_sync_port_residences(port_id, day, errors)
	if not bool(_opening_placed.get(port_id, false)):
		_opening_place_full(port_id, p, day, errors)
		_opening_placed[port_id] = true
	# FRM-SYS07-16／17: abundance／scarcity／excess before satisfaction.
	var as_state: Dictionary = _apply_abund_scarce_excess(port_id, errors)
	# ② sat targets + inertia
	_update_satisfaction(p, port_id, errors)
	_pending_sat_ports.append(port_id)
	# ③ streaks (use updated sat)
	_update_streaks(p)
	# ④ flows from S0
	var flows: Dictionary = _compute_flows(
		port_id, p, s0_poor, s0_cit, s0_rich, s0_beg, s0_sat_p, s0_sat_c, s0_sat_r, as_state
	)
	_accumulate_season_flows(season, port_id, flows)
	# ⑤ apply once
	p.beggar = p.beggar + int(flows["in"]) - int(flows["out_beg"])
	p.poor = (
		p.poor
		- int(flows["up1"])
		+ int(flows["dn1"])
		- int(flows["out_poor"])
	)
	p.citizen = (
		p.citizen
		+ int(flows["up1"])
		- int(flows["up2"])
		+ int(flows["dn2"])
		- int(flows["dn1"])
	)
	p.rich = p.rich + int(flows["up2"]) - int(flows["dn2"])
	_clamp_nonneg(p, port_id, errors)
	# FRM-03／OQ-13w: release workers whose workshops vanished (e.g. demolish).
	var lost_poor: int = _apply_lost_workshop_workers(port_id, p)
	# FRM-03／Q-07a: residents over job_cap → citizen via PF-5 → beggar (gradual).
	## A15（D-89c）: citizen→poor→beggar rewrite deferred — keep current path; see gaps.md.
	var overflow_cit: int = _apply_job_cap_overflow(port_id, p, s0_cit)
	# ⑥ hire
	var hired_poor: int = _hire_workers(port_id, p)
	# ⑦ incremental placement — movers only (FRM-SYS07-12／OQ-13j／D-63e)
	## A14（D-89c）: employed-poor leave worker deduction deferred — see gaps.md.
	_place_movers(
		port_id,
		day,
		int(flows["out_poor"]) + lost_poor,
		overflow_cit,
		int(flows["dn2"]),
		int(flows["dn1"]),
		int(flows["up1"]),
		int(flows["up2"]),
		hired_poor
	)
	_pending_pop_ports.append(port_id)
```

### PopulationModel._opening_place_full (PERF name pop.opening_place)
FILE=autoload/population_model.gd LINES=1193-1212 TOTAL_LINES=20
```gdscript
func _opening_place_full(port_id: String, p: PortPop, day: int, errors: Array) -> void:
	## FRM-SYS07-14 once: place all housed classes with FRM-SYS07-12 移入 keys (local only).
	## A13: draw_seq 0..n-1 across poor→citizen→rich; A17: bed shortfall → ERR.
	_clear_port_occupants(port_id)
	_draw_seq_by_purpose = {}
	for _i in range(p.poor):
		if not _place_one_in(port_id, day, "poor"):
			errors.append(
				_err(ERR_SYS07_09, "residence", port_id, "opening place: no bed for poor")
			)
	for _j in range(p.citizen):
		if not _place_one_in(port_id, day, "citizen"):
			errors.append(
				_err(ERR_SYS07_09, "residence", port_id, "opening place: no bed for citizen")
			)
	for _k in range(p.rich):
		if not _place_one_in(port_id, day, "rich"):
			errors.append(
				_err(ERR_SYS07_09, "residence", port_id, "opening place: no bed for rich")
			)
```

### PopulationModel._place_one_in (place_one_in)
FILE=autoload/population_model.gd LINES=1287-1309 TOTAL_LINES=23
```gdscript
func _place_one_in(port_id: String, day: int, clazz: String) -> bool:
	## 移入：weight 1/w → score = u^(1/(1/w)) = u^w among residences with free beds.
	var lambda: float = Params.get_float(PRM_LAMBDA)
	var draw_seq: int = _next_draw_seq(PURPOSE_IN)
	var best_id := ""
	var best_score: float = SCORE_SENTINEL_MIN
	for rid_v in _local_res_ids(port_id):
		var rid: String = String(rid_v)
		var r: Dictionary = _residents[rid]
		var used: int = int(r["poor"]) + int(r["citizen"]) + int(r["rich"])
		if used >= int(r["cap"]):
			continue
		var w: float = _ring_weight(int(r["ring"]), lambda)
		var u: float = _draw_u(port_id, day, rid, PURPOSE_IN, draw_seq)
		var score: float = pow(u, w)
		if score > best_score:
			best_score = score
			best_id = rid
	if best_id == "":
		return false
	var bucket: Dictionary = _residents[best_id]
	bucket[clazz] = int(bucket[clazz]) + 1
	return true
```
